# Metric formula contracts

Generated by `python -m metric_formula_contracts`; edit the catalog and contract builders, then regenerate. This inventory documents current implementation semantics. It does not certify agreement with all source papers or external software.

`implementation_contract` means source traced; `analytic_cases_checked` means the named formula family has discriminating synthetic examples, not every machine/layer combination; `research_variant_pending_review` means literature/physical interpretation still needs independent review. No row claims external validation.

Counts include container keys (four MI triplets and the motion-profile container), scalar components, native layers and research variants; they are not counts of independently validated scalar metrics. Formula versions come from `formula_versions.py`. Aurora remains explicitly unversioned research code.

Precision: validation requests full precision; legacy default output rounding remains for compatibility. Floating-point error tolerances are separate from display precision. See each row for missing values and fallback rules.

Compatibility in geometry-v4: BJAR, AXJD, AYJD, JA and SPORT dual-layer tuples flatten as `_mlcx1`/`_mlcx2`, replacing incidental `_1`/`_2` names. JA now has a catalog entry. NumPy MI triplets must flatten to `_mis`, `_mia`, `_mit` scalar components, including native-layer names; an array is a container, not a scalar metric.

Discriminating examples: disjoint 2x1 slots at [0,2] and [4,6] have geometric union4, AAV bank envelope6 and PM max-slot area2. Bank [0,1,2] yields range-normalized LSV0.5 versus max-adjacent-difference variant0. A20x5 rectangle gives EM0.4/mm versus fullP/(2A)=0.25/mm. Endpoint MCS multiplies the two endpoint means; it differs from both mean endpoint products and midpoint geometry. These cases are executable in `tests/test_metric_formula_contracts.py`.

| ethos_sas10 | SAS10 = sum_CP count(0.5+1e-8 < gap <= 10+1e-8 mm) / sum_CP count(gap > 0.5+1e-8 mm); beam-MU mean<br>Pooled open-slot count over all CPs, each endpoint counted once; no CP MU weighting. | Every CP, including endpoints, once for pooled SAS counts; CP products for MCS and CP ratios for PR.<br>Effective gap > 0.5 mm + 1e-8 mm; closure mask also used for area and envelope; physical adjacency is preserved.<br>Varian Ethos/Halcyon dual-layer MLC (legacy formula ID ethos-report-v1): physical 56 x 5 mm intersection, native 28/29 x 10 mm staggered layers; no jaw clipping. Two-sample display agreement with a second-sample plan-label caveat, not universal vendor validation. | dimensionless<br>Beam-MU mean; SAS has no CP weights; MCS and PR use Centered CP MU: w0=du0/2, wlast=du_last/2, wi=(du_prev+du_next)/2; u=BeamMU*CMW/lastCMW.<br>Unsupported layout, clipped/nonfinite geometry, invalid cumulative MU, entirely closed beam or incomplete beam coverage => None plus warning; no subset aggregation. Empty CP contributes zero MCS and PR. | ethos-report-v1<br>analytic_cases_checked<br>ethos_report_metrics.py:calculate_ethos_beam_metrics; ethos_report_metrics.py:supported_beam_layout |

| mlc_speed_acc | For each leaf, compute the proportion of valid intervals falling into each Park 2015 speed/acceleration bin; then average over leaves and beams.<br>Per-leaf bins are [lo,hi), except final [16,20] mm/s and [160,200] mm/s^2 include upper edge. Base container holds ten bins plus four summaries. | Adjacent CPs; dt=max(abs(du)/(DoseRateSet/60),shortest_abs_dtheta/machine_max_speed) over available finite timing candidates; dose-rate setting comes from the beam; unavailable candidates yield NaN.<br>All raw leaves; finite values only. Proportion denominator includes finite speeds above 20 or accelerations above 200 even though no bin contains them.<br>Native single-device apertures; dual-device base values are layer tuples before flattening; base MCSv is replaced by effective MCS5 when Halcyon paper metrics are available. | dimensionless<br>Unweighted mean across usable leaves; plan uses max(Ncp-1,1), not MU, as beam weights.<br>No valid timing/motion => NaN, serialized as unavailable rather than a physical zero. | geometry-v4<br>implementation_contract<br>ComplexityMetric/proportion_mlc_speed_acceleration.py:ProportionMLCSpeedAcceleration; ApertureMetric/mlc_attributes.py:MLCAttributes |

| lt_mean_leaf | LT Mean Leaf = total raw geometric trajectory travel / number of moving physical leaves<br>Sum raw absolute trajectory increments for each physical leaf; average only leaves with positive total travel; no MU interval weighting. | Adjacent CP raw leaf-coordinate differences; shortest absolute gantry angle modulo 360.<br>LT includes a slot unless it is outside full jaws at both endpoints. LT Mean Leaf instead tests Y-jaw overlap only; no gap clipping of trajectories.<br>Native single-device apertures; dual-device base values are layer tuples before flattening; base MCSv is replaced by effective MCS5 when Halcyon paper metrics are available. | mm/moving leaf<br>Beam-MU mean of beam moving-leaf means; no moving leaf => 0.<br>No eligible observations => 0; missing/misaligned/nonfinite/negative or zero-total MU weights => uniform weights with a warning; nonfinite result => 0. | geometry-v4<br>implementation_contract<br>vcomx_vmat_metrics.py:_leaf_travel; ComplexityMetric/aperture_series_metrics.py:mean_leaf_travel |

| ta | TA = mean projection treatment width across the sinogram<br>Per row span=last_open_index-first_open_index+1, or0; mean over all rows. | All retained sinogram projections and leaf columns; parser terminal-row policy is recorded in input metadata.<br>S>0 is open; S is fractional opening in [0,1], LOT_ms=S*projection_time_s*1000.<br>Fractional sinogram; physical planned motion comes from the parser's recorded source/fallback, not a delivery log. | leaf slots<br>Plan-level sinogram calculation; no VMAT CP-MU or beam-MU weighting.<br>Empty eligible observation set => 0 unless a specific denominator rule below states otherwise. | tomo-v3<br>implementation_contract<br>tomo_metrics.py:_geometry_metrics |

## CYBERKNIFE_MLC (6 records)

| Key | Formula / normalization | Sampling / mask / representation | Units / aggregation / missing | Version / evidence / source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| mcs | MCS = sum_segments[(MU_segment / MU_plan) * AAV_segment * LSV_segment]<br>Per segment (A/E)*LSV; E computed across all beams sharing XML interval_key. E=sum_slot max(0,max_CP raw_right-min_CP raw_left)*max_CP exposed_height, over CPs where that slot is not outside jaws; this max-slot bank envelope is not a geometric union. Each bank uses R=max(x)-min(x), not max adjacent difference; 1-sum(abs(diff(x)))/((n-1)*R); two bank scores multiply. Active indices are compressed before differences. With standard RTPLAN MLC geometry and no XML segment model, helper fallback is VMAT MCS: mean_du(mean_endpoint(A/E_beam)*mean_endpoint(LSV)), then beam-MU mean. | Delivered MLC segment apertures; XML interval_key groups the envelope; no endpoint interpolation.<br>Positive jaw-clipped gap and positive Y-jaw overlap; per-device absolute IEC leaf boundaries reflected to internal Y; area uses exposed slot height.<br>XML-associated CyberKnife MLC segments are the primary path; standard RTPLAN MLC geometry uses the explicitly described VMAT helper fallback. These paths must not be assumed numerically equivalent. | dimensionless<br>Segment-MU/planMU sum for MCS,EM,PI,LG; beam-MU/planMU sum for PM; unweighted pooled-pair ratio for SAS10.<br>PlanMU<=0 => all six0; no open gaps=>LG/SAS0; E<=0=>AAV0 and beam PM skipped; incomplete XML association limits the represented subset. | geometry-v4<br>implementation_contract<br>cyberknife_metrics.py:calculate_cyberknife_metrics; cyberknife_metrics.py:_build_union_area; analysis_helpers.py:calculate_cyberknife_mlc_metrics |
| em | EM = sum_segments[(MU_segment/MU_plan)*horizontal leaf-side boundary/A_segment], C1=0,C2=1; zero A gives 0<br>Per segment horizontal leaf-side boundary/A (C1=0,C2=1); zero A=>0; not fullP/(2*A). Standard-MLC fallback uses the same per-aperture horizontal/A ratio with centered CP-MU and beam-MU means. | Delivered MLC segment apertures; XML interval_key groups the envelope; no endpoint interpolation.<br>Positive jaw-clipped gap and positive Y-jaw overlap; per-device absolute IEC leaf boundaries reflected to internal Y; area uses exposed slot height.<br>XML-associated CyberKnife MLC segments are the primary path; standard RTPLAN MLC geometry uses the explicitly described VMAT helper fallback. These paths must not be assumed numerically equivalent. | mm^-1<br>Segment-MU/planMU sum for MCS,EM,PI,LG; beam-MU/planMU sum for PM; unweighted pooled-pair ratio for SAS10.<br>PlanMU<=0 => all six0; no open gaps=>LG/SAS0; E<=0=>AAV0 and beam PM skipped; incomplete XML association limits the represented subset. | geometry-v4<br>implementation_contract<br>cyberknife_metrics.py:calculate_cyberknife_metrics; cyberknife_metrics.py:_build_union_area; analysis_helpers.py:calculate_cyberknife_mlc_metrics |

These paths must not be assumed numerically equivalent. | dimensionless<br>Segment-MU/planMU sum for MCS,EM,PI,LG; beam-MU/planMU sum for PM; unweighted pooled-pair ratio for SAS10.<br>PlanMU<=0 => all six0; no open gaps=>LG/SAS0; E<=0=>AAV0 and beam PM skipped; incomplete XML association limits the represented subset. | geometry-v4<br>implementation_contract<br>cyberknife_metrics.py:calculate_cyberknife_metrics; cyberknife_metrics.py:_build_union_area; analysis_helpers.py:calculate_cyberknife_mlc_metrics |
| pm | PM = sum_beams[(MU_beam/MU_plan)*(1-sum_segments(MU_segment*A_segment)/(MU_beam*E_XML_interval))]; E is the raw-bank max-slot envelope across the XML interval, not geometric union<br>For each beam 1-sum_segment(mu*A)/(beamMU*E_interval); then beamMU/planMU weighted sum. Explicit beam MU is used for MU/mm; absent MU falls back to cumulative-weight span, so proxy values must not be compared as absolute MU/mm. | Adjacent CPs within each beam only; shortest signed gantry unwrap then absolute dtheta; axial dz=abs(delta z).
